</div></div></div></div><div class="panel-grid panel-has-style section-skoll-do" id="pg-177792-1"><div class="full-width panel-row-style panel-row-style-for-177792-1" id="home-feature-section"><div class="panel-grid-cell" id="pgc-177792-1-0"><div class="so-panel widget widget_text panel-first-child" data-index="1" id="panel-177792-1-0-0"><div class="mb-headline panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-177792-1-0-0"> <div class="textwidget"><div class="h-text-container"><h1><span class="bkt-white">Social Entrepreneurs</span><br/><span class="bkt-yellow">Driving Large Scale</span> <br/> <span class="bkt-yellow">Change</span></h1></div></div>
</div></div><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-headline" data-index="2" id="panel-177792-1-0-1"><div class="headline-wdg panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-177792-1-0-1"><div class="so-widget-sow-headline so-widget-sow-headline-default-3d7e4626e434"><div class="sow-headline-container">
<h1 class="sow-headline">The Skoll Foundation drives large-scale change by investing in, connecting, and celebrating social entrepreneurs and the innovators who help them solve the world’s most pressing problems.</h1> <div class="decoration">
<div class="decoration-inside"></div>
</div>
</div></div></div></div><div class="so-panel widget widget_sow-features panel-last-child" data-index="3" id="panel-177792-1-0-2"><div class="feature-skoll panel-widget-style panel-widget-style-for-177792-1-0-2"><div class="so-widget-sow-features so-widget-sow-features-default-95dfbd4ce156">
<div class="sow-features-list sow-features-responsive">
<div class="sow-features-feature sow-features-feature-last-row" style="width: 33.333%">
<div class="sow-icon-container sow-container-none" style="font-size: 70px; color: #404040; width: 70px; height: 70px;">
<div class="sow-icon-image" style="background-image: url(http://s12982.pcdn.co/wp-content/uploads/2018/02/feature_1.png); font-size: 70px"></div> </div>
<div class="textwidget">
<h5>
												Invest											</h5>
<p>We identify people and organizations with innovations creating scalable positive impact. We invest at their inflection point towards scale, our bet that these social entrepreneurs have charted a path to fundamentally transform unjust systems.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sow-features-feature sow-features-feature-last-row" style="width: 33.333%">
<div class="sow-icon-container sow-container-none" style="font-size: 70px; color: #404040; width: 70px; height: 70px;">
<div class="sow-icon-image" style="background-image: url(http://s12982.pcdn.co/wp-content/uploads/2018/02/feature_2.png); font-size: 70px"></div> </div>
<div class="textwidget">
<h5>
												Connect											</h5>
<p>Social Entrepreneurship is a team sport. We believe that change agents thrive in a well cultivated community of their peers. Scalable solutions to the world’s most pressing problems require innovative partnerships.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="sow-features-feature sow-features-feature-last-row" style="width: 33.333%">
<div class="sow-icon-container sow-container-none" style="font-size: 70px; color: #404040; width: 70px; height: 70px;">
<div class="sow-icon-image" style="background-image: url(http://s12982.pcdn.co/wp-content/uploads/2018/02/feature_3.png); font-size: 70px"></div> </div>
<div class="textwidget">
<h5>
												Celebrate											</h5>
<p>We believe a story well told can change the world. Through our Skoll Award for Social Entrepreneurship, we have supported 122 social entrepreneurs and 100 organizations on five continents. Their stories show the world what is possible. </p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

  (function trackOutbounds() {
	
  	var hitCallbackHandler = function(url, win, mailto, customized) {
      if (customized) {
        return;
      }
      if(mailto){
        window.open(url, '_self');
      }else{
        if (win) {
          window.open(url, win);
        } else {
          window.open(url, '_blank');
        }
      }
    };
    
    var addEvent = function(el, eventName, handler) {
  		if (el.addEventListener) {
  			el.addEventListener(eventName, handler);
  			} else {
  			el.attachEvent('on' + eventName, function(){
  				handler.call(el);
  		  });
  		}
  	}
	   
  	if (document.getElementsByTagName) {
  		var el = document.getElementsByTagName('a');
  		var getDomain = document.domain.split('.').reverse()[1] + '.' + document.domain.split('.').reverse()[0];
		
  		// Look thru each a element
  		for (var i=0; i < el.length;i++) {
		
  			// Extract it's href attribute
  			var href = (typeof(el[i].getAttribute('href')) == 'string' ) ? el[i].getAttribute('href') : '';
			
  			// Query the href for the top level domain (xxxxx.com)
  			var myDomain = href.match(getDomain);
			  var mailto = href.match(/^mailto\:/i);

  			// If link is outbound and is not to this domain	
  			if ((href.match(/^(https?:|\/\/)/i)  && !myDomain) || mailto) {
  				// Add an event to click
  				addEvent(el[i],'click', function(e) {
  					var url = this.getAttribute('href'), win = (typeof(this.getAttribute('target')) == 'string') ? this.getAttribute('target') : '';
            var is_mailto = url.search(/^mailto\:/i);
            var customized = this.getAttribute('customized');
            console.log(customized);
  					console.log ("add event", url);

  					ga('send', 'event', 'Outbound Link', 'Click', url,
              {'hitCallback': hitCallbackHandler(url, win, is_mailto > -1, customized)},
  						{'nonInteraction': 1}
  					);
					
  					e.preventDefault();
  				});
  			}
  		}
  	}

			function manuallyLoadStylesheet(num) {
				var link = document.createElement("link");
				link.rel = "stylesheet";
				link.href = "http://s12982.pcdn.co/wp-content/themes/skoll/style.css";
				document.getElementsByTagName("head")[0].appendChild(link);
				console.log('skoll styles manually loaded ('+num+')');
			}
			
			/* Naive check if style.css is loaded. If not, append it to the head
			 *	Intended to catch partial load style errors on weak internet connections.
			 *	(Note: naive in the sense that the theme path is hard coded here.)
			 */
			var styles = document.styleSheets; 
			var manuallyAdded = false;
			for (var i = 0; i < styles.length; i++) {
				var temp = styles[i].href;
				try{
					if (styles[i].href.match("http://s12982.pcdn.co/wp-content/themes/skoll/style.css")) {
						// File is in DOM
						if (styles[i].cssRules != null && styles[i].cssRules.length == 0) {
							// Request failed or CSS sheet invalid / empty
							if(!manuallyAdded) manuallyLoadStylesheet('1');
							manuallyAdded = true;
							break;
						} else {
							// Request successful, break
							break;
						}
					} else if (i == styles.length-1) {
						// File is not in DOM
						if(!manuallyAdded) manuallyLoadStylesheet('2');
						manuallyAdded = true;
					}
				} catch(err) {
					console.log('Error: '+err);
					if(!manuallyAdded) manuallyLoadStylesheet('3');
					manuallyAdded = true;
					console.log('Error checking for stylesheet');
				}
			}
